# Wiktionary Maltese - Letter D (Refined)

Total Entries: 833

## addebita (v-addebita)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to debit, to charge
    **MT**: Irreġistra debitu jew ikkargaw somma flus minn kont.
- **Alternative Forms**: ddebita
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Addebita b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed to debit, to charge with caution and great skill at home.

---

## dab (v-dab)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to melt (become liquid, especially through warmth)
    **MT**: Jidħol fl-istat likwidu minħabba s-sħana u l-qawwa tan-nar.
  - **EN**: to disappear
    **MT**: Għeb għalkollox jew naqas mill-viżjoni.
  - **EN**: to become emaciated
    **MT**: Isir dgħajjef u rqiq ħafna f'ġismu.
  - **EN**: to show tender feelings
    **MT**: Wera emozzjonijiet artab jew imħabba kbira.
- **Alternative Forms**: dieb
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-silġ dab meta ħarġet ix-xemx sħuna.
    EN: The ice melted when the hot sun came out.

---

## dabbar (v-dabbar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-b-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to ulcerate
    **MT**: Ikkawża jew żviluppa ferita jew dabra fil-ġilda.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dabbar b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed to ulcerate with caution and great skill at home.

---

## dabra (n-dabra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-b-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: ulcer, sore
    **MT**: Ferita miftuħa fuq il-ġilda jew ġewwa l-ġisem.
  - **EN**: tear, rent, rip
    **MT**: Tiċrita fl-ilbies jew fid-drapp.
  - **EN**: debt
    **MT**: Obbligu finanzjarju jew flus li għandhom jitħallsu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabib ikkuraha mid-dabra li kellha fuq riġilha.
    EN: The doctor treated her for the ulcer she had on her leg.

---

## Dabrani (n-dabrani)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Aldebaran (star)
    **MT**: Klassifikazzjoni jew oġġett li jikkorrispondi għal Aldebaran.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-użu ta' Dabrani kien prominenti ħafna f'din it-tradizzjoni.
    EN: The use of Aldebaran was very prominent in this tradition.

## dagħa (n-dagħa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-għ-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: swearing, cursing, blasphemy
    **MT**: L-att li tnaqqas jew tenfasizza kliem ta' stmerrija jew dagħa.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm ħafna dagħa meta beda l-argument.
    EN: There was a lot of swearing when the argument started.

## daq (v-daq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to taste (perceive the taste of)
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to taste jew iwettaqha.
  - **EN**: to taste, try (eat or drink a bit of in order to check the taste)
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to taste, try jew iwettaqha.
  - **EN**: to experience, to go through
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to experience, to go through jew iwettaqha.
- **Alternative Forms**: dieq, doq
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Daq b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed to taste with caution and great skill at home.

---

## daqna (n-daqna)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: beard in general, as a style
    **MT**: Klassifikazzjoni jew oġġett li jikkorrispondi għal beard in general, as a style.
  - **EN**: beard as an object of shaving
    **MT**: Klassifikazzjoni jew oġġett li jikkorrispondi għal beard as an object of shaving; facial hair; stubbles.
  - **EN**: facial hair
    **MT**: Klassifikazzjoni jew oġġett li jikkorrispondi għal beard as an object of shaving; facial hair; stubbles.
  - **EN**: stubbles
    **MT**: Klassifikazzjoni jew oġġett li jikkorrispondi għal beard as an object of shaving; facial hair; stubbles.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-użu ta' daqna kien prominenti ħafna f'din it-tradizzjoni.
    EN: The use of beard in general, as a style was very prominent in this tradition.

---

## daqq (n-daqq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-q-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: playing (music), ringing
    **MT**: L-att li tipproduċi mużika jew ħoss b'istrument jew qanpiena.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-daqq tal-orkestra kien meraviljuż f'dawk il-mumenti.
    EN: The playing of the orchestra was wonderful in those moments.

---

## daqq (v-daqq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-q-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to play (an instrument, a song)
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to play jew iwettaqha.
  - **EN**: to beat
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to beat jew iwettaqha.
  - **EN**: to give money
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to give money jew iwettaqha.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Daqq b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed to play with caution and great skill at home.

---

## daqqa (adv-daqqa)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: d-q-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sometimes
    **MT**: Mod jew stil li fih jiġi espress sometimes.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-espressjoni 'daqqa' b'mod xieraq fid-diskors.
    EN: Used the expression 'sometimes' appropriately in speech.

## daqs (prep-daqs)
- **POS**: preposition
- **Root**: d-q-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: as much as
    **MT**: Forma u deskrizzjoni ta' as much as; to the degree of.
  - **EN**: to the degree of
    **MT**: Forma u deskrizzjoni ta' as much as; to the degree of.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-espressjoni 'daqs' b'mod xieraq fid-diskors.
    EN: Used the expression 'as much as; to the degree of' appropriately in speech.

---

## daqshekk (adv-daqshekk)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: so much, so
    **MT**: Fl-ammont jew fil-livell kbir speċifikat.
  - **EN**: no more, that is all
    **MT**: Kelma użata biex tindika li spiċċa jew m’hemmx iżjed.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Daqshekk, il-xogħol tagħna għal-lum lest!
    EN: That's all, our work for today is finished!

---

## daqsiex (adj-daqsiex)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: ginormous, humongous
    **MT**: Kwalità jew karatteristika ta' oġġett jew persuna li tkun ginormous, humongous.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm oġġett daqsiex f'nofs il-kamra.
    EN: There was a ginormous, humongous object in the middle of the room.

---

## daqskemm (conj-daqskemm)
- **POS**: conjunction
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: as much as, as... as

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-espressjoni 'daqskemm' b'mod xieraq fid-diskors.
    EN: Used the expression 'as much as, as... as' appropriately in speech.

---

    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to turn; to veer; to change direction jew iwettaqha.

    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to wander; to walk about; to travel jew iwettaqha.

  - **EN**: to travel

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dar b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed to turn; to veer; to change direction with caution and great skill at home.

    **MT**: Tawwal il-ħin jew ittardja azzjoni jew vjaġġ.

    **MT**: Ħalla lil persuna tistenna għal żmien twil.
- **Alternative Forms**: dewwem
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dawwem b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed to delay with caution and great skill at home.

## daxkien (intj-daxkien)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Used to express surprise or curiosity about an unexpected event. Roughly translates to: “Really?”
    **MT**: Forma u deskrizzjoni ta' Used to express surprise or curiosity about an unexpected event. Roughly translates to: “Really?”; “What's the occasion?”; “What’s going on here?”.
  - **EN**: “What's the occasion?”
    **MT**: Forma u deskrizzjoni ta' Used to express surprise or curiosity about an unexpected event. Roughly translates to: “Really?”; “What's the occasion?”; “What’s going on here?”.
  - **EN**: “What’s going on here?”
    **MT**: Forma u deskrizzjoni ta' Used to express surprise or curiosity about an unexpected event. Roughly translates to: “Really?”; “What's the occasion?”; “What’s going on here?”.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-espressjoni 'daxkien' b'mod xieraq fid-diskors.
    EN: Used the expression 'Used to express surprise or curiosity about an unexpected event. Roughly translates to: “Really?”; “What's the occasion?”; “What’s going on here?”' appropriately in speech.

- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: derogative
    **MT**: Kwalità jew karatteristika ta' oġġett jew persuna li tkun derogative.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm oġġett derogattiv f'nofs il-kamra.
    EN: There was a derogative object in the middle of the room.

---

## derra (v-derra)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-r-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to thresh or winnow (separate the grain from the chaff, regardless of manner)
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to thresh or winnow jew iwettaqha.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Derra b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed to thresh or winnow with caution and great skill at home.

---

    EN: Used the expression 'decillion' appropriately in speech.

## dieb (v-dieb)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to melt (become liquid, especially through warmth)
    **MT**: Jidħol fl-istat likwidu minħabba s-sħana u l-qawwa tan-nar.
  - **EN**: to disappear
    **MT**: Għeb għalkollox jew naqas mill-viżjoni.
  - **EN**: to become emaciated
    **MT**: Isir dgħajjef u rqiq ħafna f'ġismu.
  - **EN**: to show tender feelings
    **MT**: Wera emozzjonijiet artab jew imħabba kbira.
- **Alternative Forms**: dab
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dieb b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed dieb with caution and great skill at home.

---

## iddiskuta (v-iddiskuta)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to discuss
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' kif wieħed to discuss jew iwettaqha.
- **Alternative Forms**: ddiskuta
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Iddiskuta b'kawtela u b'sengħa kbira fid-dar.
    EN: He performed iddiskuta with caution and great skill at home.

---

## Ċimber (n-ċimber)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: December
    **MT**: Klassifikazzjoni jew oġġett li jikkorrispondi għal December.
- **Alternative Forms**: Diċembru
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-użu ta' Ċimber kien prominenti ħafna f'din it-tradizzjoni.
    EN: The use of Ċimber was very prominent in this tradition.

---

## ġiġà (adv-ġiġà)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: already
    **MT**: Mod jew stil li fih jiġi espress already.
- **Alternative Forms**: diġà
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Uża l-espressjoni 'ġiġà' b'mod xieraq fid-diskors.
    EN: Used the expression 'ġiġà' appropriately in speech.

---
